中砂 (1560 TT)  Trading Buy

先進製程、先進封裝帶動中砂的鑽石碟的需求，投資建議
給 Trading Buy 。  
投資建議 ：先進製程、先進封裝帶動中砂的鑽石碟的需求，中
砂持續擴充鑽石碟的產能，帶動獲利、評價提升，投資建議給
Trading Buy 。  
中砂 2Q26 獲利大幅年增： 中砂 2Q26 營收 24.93 億元，
QoQ+8.70%/YoY+17.87% ；稅後純益 5.23 億元，
QoQ+19.94%/YoY+96.49% ；稅後 EPS 3.46 元。 2Q26 業外利益
包含投資獲利。  
中砂 2Q26 事業群營運狀況： ABU：受惠於傳產機械與工具機砂
輪需求回溫。 DBU ：受惠於 CMP 鑽石碟於 2.0/1.6nm 製程出貨
持續上升， 2nm 鑽石碟營收增加 84%、1.4nm 鑽石碟開始出貨。
SBU ： 12 吋月產能 36 萬片滿載。  
中砂 2026 年各事業群展望： 傳統砂輪事業部 (ABU) ：2026 年將
受惠需求回溫及新砂輪子公司合併綜效，目標營收較 2025 雙位
數以上成長。  
鑽石碟事業部 (DBU) ：先進製程及先進封裝之鑽石碟需求將逐
季持續增加，美系記憶體及 IDM 客戶，中砂將持續增加市占率
及逐季放量。  
晶圓事業部 (SBU) ：2027 年將增加至 12 吋的月產能到 43 萬片，
高階產品組合比重將持續提高，以提升營收及毛利率。  
群益預估中砂 2026 年 /2027 年稅後 EPS 14.52 元 /21.06 元：群
益預估中砂 2026 年 /2027 年營收為 101.87 億元 /135.79 億元，
YoY+25.00%/+33.30% ；稅後純益 21.97 億元 /31.87 億元，
YoY+61.48%/+45.07% ；稅後 EPS 14.52 元 /21.06 元。

### Page 2

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 9 月 23 日

2 群益投顧 
  ESG ：  
E：(Environmental) 。中砂秉持企業經營須兼顧環境保護之理念，持續落實環境
友善管理策略與措施。國際永續趨勢與國家 2050 淨零路徑推動下，中砂透過
執行組織型溫室氣體盤查、產品碳足跡盤查及導入循環經濟專案，循序漸進達
成減碳目標。  
此外，中砂重視氣候變遷伴隨之風險與機會，亦將氣候行動納入公司風險管理
與營運策略，積極研擬因應策略及應變措施；對於水資源、空氣污染、廢棄物
等環境議題，更推行各項管理與減量方案，朝向永續經營之願景邁進。  
S： (Social) 。中砂長期深耕地方社區創生與投入藝文教育與活動，使參與社會
公益成為中砂的企業文化。秉持著「共好：你好、我好、大家好」的經營理念
及懷著「知恩、感恩、報恩」的心，中砂持續與當地縣市政府、學校、社福機
構團體與自身基金會合作，攜手保留珍貴產業文化，提倡書法教育，為當地社
區帶來實質性的協助與回饋，發揮企業正向影響力。  
中砂座落於台灣著名的陶瓷之鄉 — 鶯歌，期望與鄉里共榮，為社區民眾打造友
善的生活環境，因此長期參與社區關懷與愛鄉感恩公益，連續多年贊助新北市
鶯歌區西鶯里、中湖里的敬老活動與銀髮族共餐活動，並致贈年節禮盒及物資
予鄰里長輩。近年中砂更與民間公益培訓單位合作，透過「學生提案，企業協
力」的方式，與公益團體合作推出聯名禮盒、愛心送餐等創新專案。除了達到
支援公益團體的目的外，中砂更將聯名禮盒分送給鶯歌的鄰里長輩，持續傳遞
公益善心。  
G： (Governance) 。企業永續發展的關鍵建基在健全且完善的公司治理架構上，
使得強化董事會監督管理職能成為中砂精進公 司治理的核心目標。除了保障股
東權益外，中砂更積極建構具備應對傳統及氣候變遷、資訊安全等新興風險的
管理機制，透過完整的鑑別、評估及擬定因應措施的流程，降低潛在風險對公
司營運之衝擊。在持續提升營運韌性的同時，也為利害關係人創造更大的價值。
公司簡介：  
中國砂輪股份有限公司成立於 07/1964 ，總部位於鶯歌，主要從事生產及銷售
砂輪、鑽石碟、再生晶圓等研磨工具。  
公司早期產品以應用於石材加工的傳統砂輪為主，後因取得鑽石碟技術的專利
授權及台系大客戶供應鏈在地化策略，以鑽石碟產品切入半導體產業領域。
2020 年，公司再生晶圓事業部竹科廠於竹南科 學園區落成啟用量產，成為全球
前三大再生晶圓供應商。  
再生晶圓主要台灣同業包含辛耘 (3583 TT) 、昇陽半導體 (8028 TT) ；鑽石碟為
3M(MMM US) 、Abrasive Technology 、Asahi(4216 JP) 、Seasol( 韓 )等。中砂大客
戶包含台系晶圓代工廠、美系記憶體廠等。  
中砂自 04/2025 開始認列日本三井金屬礦業集團旗下的兩家公司 MKS 與 MGT，
歸類在傳統砂輪事業部 (ABU) 。  
營收占比：  
傳統砂輪事業部 (ABU) ，毛利率 20~25% 。用於工具機、機械，近期還有包含半
導體 (碳化矽 )的產業。

3 群益投顧 
鑽石碟事業部 (DBU) ，毛利率 45% 以上。用於成熟製程、先進製程、先進封裝。
單一客戶營收占比超過 54% 。  
晶圓事業部 (SBU) ，毛利率 30~35% 。用於半導體製程，單價約 10~20 美金。

資料來源：中砂法說簡報。  
中砂 2Q26 事業群營運狀況：  
ABU ：受惠於傳產機械與工具機砂輪需求回溫。 DBU ：受惠於 CMP 鑽石碟於
2.0/1.6nm 製程出貨持續上升， 2nm 鑽石碟營收增加 84%、1.4nm 鑽石碟開始出
貨。 SBU ： 12 吋月產能 36 萬片滿載。  
2026 年 CAPEX 與擴產計畫：  
中砂 2026 年 CAPEX 約 15 億元， 4Q26 鑽石碟月產能預計增加至 7.0 萬顆、
4Q27 增加至 9.5 萬顆； 4Q26 12 吋再生晶圓月產能將增加至 40 萬片， 8 吋約
15 萬片，共 55 萬片。  
資料來源：中砂法說簡報。  
中砂 2026 年各事業群展望：  
傳統砂輪事業部 (ABU) ： 2026 年將受惠需求回溫及新砂輪子公司合併綜效，目
標營收較 2025 雙位數以上成長。  
鑽石碟事業部 (DBU) ：先進製程及先進封裝之鑽石碟需求將逐季持續增加，美
系記憶體及 IDM 客戶，中砂將持續增加市占率及逐季放量。  
晶圓事業部 (SBU) ： 2027 年將增加至 12 吋的月產能到 43 萬片，高階產品組合
比重將持續提高，以提升營收及毛利率。

7 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 9 日

1 群益投顧 
ASML (ASML US) 與台積電 (2330 TT) 共同推動 High NA EUV 將自現行的 6 吋光罩，
轉移至更大的 12 吋 光 罩。 目標於 2031 年之前建立 12 吋 光 罩 試 產 線(pilot line) ， 並 於
2033 年之前實現微影系統全面就緒。台積電並表示自 2030 年 起 ， 將 在 先 進 製 程 量 產
中導入 ASML 的 High NA  EUV 。台積電與 ASML 共同發起 6 吋光罩轉移至 12 吋光
罩 的 規 格 轉 移 ， 其 戰 略 意 義 在 於 ， 製 訂 規 格 和 標 準 ， 確 保 台 積 電 技 術 領 先 地 位 。 因 此
對 台 積 電 的 投 資 評 等 Buy ，目標價 3,200 元。

研 究 員 ： 陳 俐 妍   
ly.c@capital.com.tw

ASML 與 台 積 電 共 同 推 動 High NA EUV 將 自 現 行 的 6 吋 光 罩 ， 轉 移 至 更 大
的 12 吋 光 罩 ：  
09/07/2026 ASML 與 台 積 電 於 BACUS 國 際 光 學 工 程 學 會(SPIE) 先 進 微 影 技
術 研 討 會 前 宣 布 發 起 產 業 合 作 ， 共 同 推 動High NA EUV 將 自 現 行 的 6 吋光
罩 ， 轉 移 至 更 大 的 12 吋 光 罩 ， 預 期 將 進 一 步 提 升 晶 圓 廠 的 生 產 力 、 降 低 晶
片 製 造 成 本 ， 並 消 除 需 要 2 張 6 吋 光 罩 在 High NA EUV 曝 光 時 進 行 拼 接 的
限 制，使 整 個 半 導 體 產 業 受 惠。此 轉 移 能 讓 先 進 晶 片 製 造 服 務 提 供 者 充 分 利
用 High NA EUV 的 優 勢 ， 更 提 升 未 來 世 代 先 進 晶 片 的 成 本 效 益 。 
目標於 2031 年 之 前 建 立 12 吋 光 罩 試 產 線(pilot line) ，並於 2033 年 之 前 實
現 微 影 系 統 全 面 就 緒 ， 以 支 援 先 進 製 程 量 產 。 台 積 電 並 表 示 自2030 年 起 ，
將 在 先 進 製 程 量 產 中 導 入 ASML 的 High NA 技 術 。  
12 吋光罩 使 High NA 效 率 提 升 ：  
現行 EUV 設備的 NA(Numerical Aperture ， NA) 為 0.33 ， High NA 則 提 高 到
0.55 。 NA 數 值 越 高 ， 解 析 度 也 越 高 、 曝 光 時 能 呈 現 的 細 節 愈 多 。ASML 資
料顯示 High NA 可 將 最 小 解 析 尺 寸 從 現 行 EUV 的約 13 奈 米 進 一 步 縮 小 到
8 奈 米 ， 也 就 是 能 在 晶 圓 上 印 出 更 細 微 的 電 路 圖 形 。 部 分 原 本 需 要2 次 甚 至
更多次曝光才能完成的圖形，也有機會改以單次曝光處理，降低製程複雜
度 、 缺 陷 風 險 與 生 產 周 期 。 
舉 例 來 說 ，0.33 NA EUV 機 台 ， 至 少 需 要 4 張 光 罩 、 分 多 次 曝 光 才 能 完 成
A14 、 A10 金 屬 層 圖 形 ；High NA EUV ， 只 要 1 張 光 罩 就 能 完 成 相 同 圖 形 。
但 解 析 度 提 高 ，High NA 的 光 學 設 計 讓 單 次 可 曝 光 範 圍 縮 小 到 原 本EUV 的
50% ， 製 作 大 顆 晶 片 時 可 能 必 須 分 成2 次 曝 光 ， 再 把 圖 形 拼 接 起 來 。 這 也 是
台積電與 ASML 此 次 推 動 12 吋 光 罩 的 原 因 ， 相 較 現 行 6 吋 光 罩 ， 放 大 光 罩
尺 寸 預 期 能 進 一 步 提 升 晶 圓 廠 生 產 力、降 低 晶 片 製 造 成 本，並 消 除 High NA
曝 光 時 的 拼 接 限 制 。 
台 積 電 預 計 2030 年 開 始 使 用 High NA  EUV ， 推估 High NA 將 會 在 A10 或
A10 以 下 的 先 進 製 程 採 用：  
台積電預計 2030 年 開 始 導入 High NA  EUV ，比 12 吋 光 罩 微 影 系 統 預 計 全
面就緒的 2033 年 更 早 。 這 代 表 台 積 電 初 期 導 入High NA 時 ， 仍 會 先 使 用 現
行 6 吋 光 罩 ， 之 後 再 隨 12 吋 光 罩 與 相 關 系 統 成 熟 逐 步 轉 換 。

### Page 2

產業速報

2 群益投顧 
根 據 台 積 電 目 前 公 開 的 製 程 藍 圖 ，A13 與 A12 製 程 都 預 計 2029 年 量 產 。 其
中 A13 是 A14 平 台 的 延 伸 ，A12 則 將 超 級 電 軌(Super Power Rail) 背面供電
技術導入 A14 平 台 。 因 此 N2 、 A16 、 A14 、 A13 與 A12 都 早 於 台 積 電 預 計
2030 年 導 入 High NA ， 推 估 High NA 將 會 在 A10 或 A10 以 下 的 先 進 製 程 採
用。  
Intel (INTC US) 和 Samsung (005930 KS) 領 先 台 積 電 採 用 High NA EUV ，但
也 同 時 參 與 12 吋 光 罩 開 發 ：  
Intel 在 2024 年 宣 布 完 成 全 球 首 套 商 用 High NA EUV 設 備 的 組 裝 ；2026 年
High NA 已 經 正 式 進 入 量 產 ， 並 用 於 部 分 18A 製 程 層 。 是 目 前 High NA 導
入 進 度 最 快 的 晶 片 製 造 商 。Samsung 則預計 2028 年導入 DRAM 量 產 ； 台 積
電選擇在 2030 年 導 入 。Intel 和 Samsung 也 同 時 表 示 與 ASML 及 供 應 鏈 合
作 ， 推 動 未 來 轉 向 大 尺 寸 光 罩 。 
台 積 電 與 ASML 共 同 發 起 6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋 光 罩 的 規 格 轉 移，將 領 導 業
界 規 格 ：  
6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋光罩 的 這 項 升 級 非 單 純 的 尺 寸 放 大 ， 而 是 面 臨 生 態 系
與 物 理 極 限 的 多 重 挑 戰 。 整 體 供 應 鏈 重 構 ， 包 括 基 板 與 材 料 以 及 設 備 等 等 。
12 吋 光 罩 石 英 基 板 的 平 坦 度 、 缺 陷 密 度 與 熱 膨 脹 係 數 控 制 難 度 倍 增 。 製 程
設 備 如 薄 膜 沉 積、蝕 刻、清 洗 與 自 動 化 搬 運 設 備 皆 需 重 新 開 發。後 續 檢 測 與
量測極限檢測難度也增加，更大面積的圖形缺陷檢測（ Inspection ）與修補
技 術 需 要 突 破 。 在 12 吋 面 積 上 維 持 奈 米 級 的 對 位 精 度 與 線 寬 均 勻 性 極 為 困
難   
電 子 設 計 自 動 化 (EDA) 工 具 必 須 重 新 適 應 大 尺 寸 曝 光 視 場 與 資 料 處 理 。 12 
吋 光 罩 的 圖 形 檔 案 與 運 算 複 雜 度 將 大 幅 上 升 。 
台積電與 ASML 共 同 發 起 6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋 光 罩 的 規 格 轉 移，其 戰略意
義在於 ，台 積 電 為 全 球 最 大 晶 圓 代 工 廠，為 先 進 製 程 最 大 的 供 應 商，可 以 領
導 光 罩 基 底 供 應 商 、 光 罩 檢 測 設 備 商， 甚 至 EDA 廠 等 整 個 生 態 系 ，除 共同
分攤 ， 跨 越 升 級 12 吋 光 罩 的 龐 大 研 發 成 本外 ， 製 訂 規 格 和 標 準 ， 確 保 台 積
電 技 術 領 先 地 位 。因 此 對 台 積 電 的投 資 評 等 Buy ， 目 標 價 3,200 元。

3 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

營收走勢圖

研究員/陳晏平 TINA.YP.CHEN@uni-psg.com

1H26 主要認列案源為中和 K Tower Y09 商辦案、中和心禾匯住宅
案、台北信義豪宅案持續去化與遞延交屋，1H26 累積營收為 132 億
元 ， 成長31%YOY， 且在整體毛利率較2025 年同期些微提升下 ，1H26
累積稅後淨利為 11.1 億元，成長 61%YOY，EPS 為 1.87 元。展望
2H26，兩個台中新完工建案(合計總銷金額 83 億元)遞延至 4Q26 才
正式推出銷售，且台中市地區為 2026 年以來銷售速度較慢的區域，
預期此兩個台中建案在 2026 年認列案量較少，主要將貢獻在 2027
年。所幸，近幾個月財富效果開始顯現，進而有提升居住品質、資
產配置需求，台北市信義豪宅案 1H26 已認列逾 10 億元，銷售優於
預期 。 因此 ， 研究部預估2026 年合併營收為 268 億元 ，成長 17%YOY，
稅後淨利 20 億元，成長 27%YOY，EPS 為 3.24 元，獲利將自谷底回
升；並預估 2027 年合併營收為 301 億元，成長 12%YOY，稅後淨利
為 24 億元，成長 21%YOY，EPS 為 3.92 元，營運將呈現穩增。投資
建議方面，雖目前房市相關政策已不再緊縮，並朝向逐步鬆綁，加
上高科技產業景氣成長強勁、股市上漲帶來財富效果，使得房市氣
氛將逐漸走出谷底，但因研究部預期冠德 2026~2027 年獲利皆將落
在 3~4 元之譜，未明顯跳增，近兩年現金殖利率亦約當 4~5%，未明
顯高於同業，故維持給予0.5xNAV，目標價40 元，潛在上漲空間有
限，重申中立建議。

重點摘要： 
1. 1H26認列案源主要為帳上成屋持續去化，累積稅後 EPS為1.87
元： 
受惠:(1) 中和K Tower Y09( 原中和秀朗橋捷運聯開商辦案，
總銷金額31億元，銷售率30%)新完工建案開始交屋；(2) 中和
心禾匯(原中和LG08捷運聯開住宅案，總銷金額16億元，銷售
率80%)、 台北市冠德信義豪宅案持續去化與遞延交屋入帳 ；(3)
轉投資根基營造合併營收貢獻大增75%QOQ、52%YOY至61億元，
進而帶動2Q26單季合併 營收明顯成長72%QOQ、57%YOY至84億
元。另在營造子公司有個案結算以及高毛利率冠德信義豪宅案
認列較多下 ， 毛利率亦有所提升，2Q26單季毛利率上揚至28%，
季增4個百分點、年增5個百分點，再扣除費用、所得稅負、根
基少數股權影響，2Q26單季稅後淨利為8億元(歸屬母公司)，
跳增160%QOQ、224%YOY，單季EPS為1.35元。

合計1H26累積營收為132億元，成長31%YOY，且在整體毛利率
較2025年同期些微提升下，1H26累積稅後淨利為11.1億元，成
長61%YOY，EPS為1.87元。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

2. 兩個台中新完工建案遞延至4Q26才正式推出銷售， 預估2026年
稅後EPS將自谷底回升至3.24元： 
展望2H26，除帳上成屋案持續去化外，冠德 規劃10~11月將陸
續推出台中裕毛屋住宅案(總銷金額67億元)、 台中G5捷運聯開
商辦案(總銷金額16億元)、台北市萬華直興住宅 案(總銷金額
36億元)，合計新推出案量共119億元。其中，台中裕毛屋住宅
案已於2026年6月取得使照，並有先行潛銷，預計2026年10月
正式推出銷售 ；台中G5捷運聯開商辦案亦已取得使照，計畫
2026年11月開始銷售 ，此兩案皆 將視銷售成績在2H26交屋入
帳。

考量:(1) 台中為2026年以來房屋銷售較慢的區域，且台中裕
毛屋住宅案、台中G5捷運聯開商辦案皆延至4Q26才開始銷售，
預期2026年認列比例將較原先預期少，推估將分別認列 20%、
10%案量；(2) 中和心禾匯、中和K Tower Y09可於2026年順利
銷售並完成交屋25%、1/3案量；(3) 財富效果顯現，進而有提
升居住品質、資產配置需求，預期台北市冠德信義豪宅案可去
化20億元金額，較2025年認列金額倍增。再加計營造、百貨事
業營收貢獻 ， 預估2026年合併營收為268億元 ，成長17%YOY，稅
後淨利20億元，成長27%YOY，EPS為3.24元，將自谷底回升。

3. 2027年將有一台北市住宅案完工 ，加計帳上成屋去化， 預估
2027年稅後EPS將穩增至3.92元： 
展望2027年，除持續銷售前述個案外，預期4Q27還將新推出南
港台電公辦都更住宅案，該案總銷金額達130億元，將視銷售
情況於2028年完工之時認列。而在建案認列方面，預期將有總
銷金額36億元的台北市萬華直興住宅案於3Q27完工 ， 研究部採
保守原則，推估此案在2027年將可順利去化並認列30%案量，
加計:(1)中和心禾匯、中和K Tower Y09、台中裕毛屋住宅案、
台中G5捷運聯開商辦案 ， 推估將分別去化 、 認列15%、1/3、60%、
60%案量 ；(2)台北市冠德信義豪宅案遞延交屋與持續去化20億
元；(3) 營造 、 百貨事業營收貢獻， 預估2027年合併營收為301
億元，成長12%YOY，稅後淨利為24億元，成長21%YOY，EPS為
3.92元，營運將呈現穩增。

4. 帳上土地庫存充足，估計每股資產價值為77元，目前股價評價
約0.5xNAV： 
在扣除2027年完工建案後，帳上土地庫存仍有2.2萬坪，換算
總銷金額為801億元 ， 未來推案無虞 。 就區域分布上 ， 以台北市
佔比最高，高達62%，其次為高雄市、台中市，佔比各為15%、
14%，另有板橋佔比9%。主要個案包含:板橋官邸公辦都更住宅
案、南港台電公辦都更住宅案、台北市行二行三公辦都更案、

5. 預期2027年現金殖利率將在4.4~4.9%區間水準： 
股利政策方面，為保留資本，以提高自有資金比例、降低營運
槓桿，近幾年在正常獲利情況下，現金股利配發率皆落在
35~41%區間水準 。 而因2025年獲利衰退較多 ，2026年配發1.616
元現金股利 ， 且無股票股利 ， 現金股利配發率提升至62%， 已於
2026/8/10除息，除息前現金殖利率4.7%。由於2026年獲利將
自谷底回升，研究部預期2027年現金股利將落在1.6~1.8元區
間水準，換算現金殖利率4.4~4.9%。

附錄 一 ： 評 等 之 標 準 
評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

### Page 3

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Quanta 
We are Neutral-rated on Quanta. Our 12-month target price of NT$299 is based on an 
10.7x 2027E P/E, which references PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E multiple 
correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s performance.  
Key upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market 
recovery; 2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for general servers.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
What’s new? ITEQ (6213.TW, not covered) reported August 2026 profit per TWSE
regulatory requirements:
• August revenue was NT$5,044M (+6% m/m, +98% y/y). July/August
revenues accounted for 67% of the consensus 3Q revenue estimate.
• August pre-tax profit was NT$1,302M (+762% y/y), 52% of the consensus 3Q
forecast.
• August net profit was NT$924M (+853% y/y), 55% of the consensus 3Q

estimate.
Our thoughts: ITEQ is a leading CCL supplier, with infrastructure applications
(servers, switches, telecom, etc.) accounting for ~73% of 2Q26 revenue. We view its
August earnings as a positive read-through for EMC, TUC and KB Laminates within
our coverage. The margin improvement is consistent with effective cost pass-
through and potentially wider price-cost spreads, which could continue to support
further profitability expansion going forward. We remain OW on all three names.

Key Takeaways
August pre-tax and net margins reached 25.8% and 18.3%, respectively, vs. 15.9%
and 11.1% in 2Q26.
With July/August revenue broadly tracking quarterly consensus, we think
August’s outsized earnings contribution points to upside risk to 3Q profit
expectations.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

Formosa Petrochemical (6505.TW)
Upgrade to Buy: Superior Margin on Tanker Fleet + High Export Ratio
CITI'S TAKE 
We upgrade FPCC from Neutral to Buy reflecting its unique exposure with 
in-house VLCC shipping fleet covering ~60-70% of oil transportation (least 
impacted by recent spot freight rate spike) and highest diesel export ratio 
( ~85% vs. 60% for S-Oil/SKI and 5-10% for TOP/SPRC). We see manageable 
impact from CPC’s pump price freeze (minimal margin squeeze at ~$80/bbl 
Dubai) while FPCC is also a beneficiary of any potential US diesel export 
restriction (Iink). We expect FPCC to offer the highest 26-27E dividend yield 
( ~ 7-8%) among 4 Formosa sisters and a premium valuation seems justified 
vs. Asian peers. We think oil product tightness might take longer to resolve 
given extensive Russia refinery outages. New TP at NT$108 (from NT$40). 
Quantifying impact of domestic price cap — Taiwan’s CPC indicates its retail price 
absorption reached NT$22bn cumulative YTD (Fig.1) vs. NT$17bn in end-Jun and we 
estimate ~NT$7bn in Sep alone (similar to Mar peak). FPCC has 22% Taiwan market 
share and 2025 domestic gasoline & diesel volume was 35k/17k b/d or 12% of CDU 
runs subject to price control (vs. ~18% for S-Oil/SKI, 35% for Thai peers). Assuming 
oil price stays at $100 until end-4Q, we estimate 2H26 domestic forgone EBIT could 
be similar to 1H ( ~NT$5bn) or ~US$2/bbl overall GRM drag. But this was more than 
compensated by SG product crack strength with 2025 gasoline/diesel/jet export 
ratios of 59%/86%/99%, respectively.   
Buffered from current $20+ spot VLCC rate — FPCC is one of the very few refiners 
that owns vessels (through 20% stake in Formosa Plastics Marine that has 6 VLCCs). 
We expect it could enjoy stable freight cost under LT time chartering. We also see 
lower concern on crude sourcing from SoH disruption in 3Q noting Jul/Aug refinery 
runs were 88%/92%. But we see pullback into 4Q (mid-70%) on maintenance. 
Improved olefin competitiveness from 2027 — We expect FPCC could raise cracker 
utilisation next year (33% average since 2Q) after partial conversion to ethane feed 
(up to ~400kt pa usage or ~20% of output) with minimal capex. 
Implications — Our revised TP of NT$108 is based on historical peak 2.3x P/BV (core 
book) at ~50% premium to Korean peer S-Oil given limited free float and MTM for 
affiliates (its 7.9% stake in Nanya Tech is worth NT$14/share based on market price). 
Our sensitivity analysis suggests a $5 diesel crack rise vs. our mid-$30/bbl base 
case will raise FY27E EPS by ~10%. 
n

In Taiwan, state-owned Chinese Petroleum Corp (CPC) announces retail price
adjustment on a weekly basis based on government’s fuel pricing formula (linked to
70% Dubai / 30% Brent crude basket adjusted for exchange rate) but with higher
absorption amount as oil goes higher. FPCC tends to follow suit. CPC’s domestic
price absorption was minimal in Jun-Jul’26 when Dubai price falls to ~$80/bbl or

below. However, there was a price freeze last week despite crude basket surged
another $10/bbl WoW to $126/bbl.

### Page 7

Formosa Petrochemical (6505.TW)
25 September 2026 Citi Research
7
Formosa Petrochemical
Company description
Formosa Petrochemical (FPCC) is part of the Formosa Group in Taiwan. It
owns a 540,000 b/d refinery, 2.9m tonnes of ethylene capacity from three
naphtha crackers, and a 2,800MW cogeneration power plant. It was set up
when the Taiwan government approved expansion of the private sector into
the oil refining and upstream chemical business.
Investment strategy

We rate Formosa Petrochemical stock a Buy. Despite a likely gradual GRM
normalisation ahead (dependent on Middle East / Russia supply disruptions),
we expect oil product tightness might take longer to resolve vs. crude oil
market. Its gasoline/diesel export ratio was the highest among Asian peers,
which should more than buffer margin squeeze from domestic pump control.
FPCC was the least impacted by recent spot freight rate spike given in-house
VLCC shipping fleet. While we remain concerned about an L-shaped ethylene

cycle in the next 2-3 years, FPCC could enjoy better cost competitiveness as
~20% of olefin cracker feedstock will shift to ethane from early-2027 with
minimal capex. The LT refining outlook could remain solid due to permanent
closures in the US/Europe. Healthy dividend yield (likely to be the highest
among 4 Formosa sisters in FY26E), stable utility earnings and the stock's
relatively low free float (15%) vs. its peers could provide further support.

Valuation
Our NT$108 target price for Formosa Petrochemical is based on historical
peak-cycle 2.3x FY27E P/BV with adjusted book value excluding valuation
gain of affiliates, ~50% premium to Korean peer S-Oil implied from our
TP given limited free float and MTM for affiliates. We use P/BV as our
valuation methodology due to the asset-heavy nature of the business, and
factor in its scarcity premium vs, regional peers as the only energy play in
Taiwan.

Risks
Key downside risks that could impede the shares from reaching our target
price include: 1) weaker-than-expected oil and/or chemical demand; and 2)
Taiwan government intervention on CPC's oil products pricing mechanism.
Key upside risks are: 1) extended refinery outages in the Middle East / Russia,
2) stronger global oil demand growth; 3) robust chemical re-stocking in
China; and 4) a short-term spike in refining margins on any unscheduled
shutdowns or for weather-related reasons (e.g., cold winter).

Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.
Privately held companies:
The legal entities through which Citi conducts its business do not permit investment research recommendations, ratings,

not guaranteed. Private companies are generally not subject to reporting obligations, which may result in less publicly available
information. The information contained in this report may differ from information a private company may subsequently make

Verena Jeng 
+852-2978-1681 | verena.jeng@gs.com

Pegatron (4938.TW): AI servers in expansion; New smartphone model 
with Broadband entering peak season to support growth ahead; Sell
28 September 2026 | 10:33AM HKT
3-month revenues preview: We expect sequential revenue growth in Sep-Dec, 
mainly driven by AI server racks ramp up with higher ASP and major smartphone 
brand customer’s new model launches (report link). Overall, we project Pegatron’s 
3Q/4Q26E revenues to grow +18%/+25% YoY, driven by: (1) new model launches 
from a major smartphone customer supporting September and 4Q26 revenue 
growth; (2) a continuing ramp-up in AI server shipments, across rack-level and 
baseboard-based, riding on the growing demand in Neocloud and enterprises, (3) 
broadband, IoT, and game console entering peak season. We are positive on 
Pegatron’s expansion from consumer electronics to AI servers and automotive 
electronics, while the high memory costs could cap PC and smartphone market 
growth. Maintain Sell on stretched valuation.

Pegatron’s Aug revenue was up 34% YoY / 3% MoM to NT$88bn, or 12% lower than 
our estimate. This reflects that high memory costs continue to cap PC and 
smartphone growth, despite a strong ramp-up in AI servers. Aug PC shipments was 
down 49% YoY to 0.40-0.45m, reflecting the impact of consumption pull in under 
the high memory costs. Its major smartphone brand customer launched new models 
in Sep, supporting Pegatron’s growth in Sep and 4Q26; prior to September, the 
company suffered from model transition (i.e. declining demand for older models 
while new models were in a slow shipment ramp-up phase).

Earnings revision: We factor in Pegatron’s Aug revenue and our 2026-28E net income 
largely unchanged. We cut our 2026-28E revenues by 1% / 1% / 1% to reflect the miss in 
consumer electronics under high memory costs. Our 2026-28E GM, OPM and NM are 
largely unchanged.

Valuation: We continue to derive our 12M TP based on near-term P/E, and our target 
P/E is updated to 10.8x 2027E EPS, (vs. 10.6x previously), derived from the refreshed 
peers correlation of forward year earnings growth and P/E.  With the updated earnings 
and target multiple, our 12M TP is revised up to NT$76.9 (vs. NT$75.5 previously). The 
target P/E of 10.8x is within the company’s historical avg. and avg.-1std forward trading 
P/E. Maintain Sell.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Pegatron 
We are Sell rated on Pegatron with a 12-month target price of NT$76.9. Our TP is based 
on a 10.8x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E multiple 
correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s share performance.  
Key risks: 1) stronger-than-expected smartphone market recovery, 2) 
faster-than-expected AI server ramp-up, and 3) stronger-than-expected demand on 
consumer electronics.

on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

## Document 081: 260928_ubs_TSMC

Source PDF: all report pdf/260928_ubs_TSMC.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-28
Pages: 19
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

ab 28 September 2026
Global Research
Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing
Q326 earnings preview: Raising 2027-28E capex
for next phase of AI growth
Cloud AI mega-trend driving sales growth >40% in 2026E and 40% in 2027E
Feedback from our recent industry analysis suggests no signs of Cloud AI demand
moderation (Summit Day 1, Day 2&3, CoWoS update); yet the demand outlook has
further strengthened across CPUs, GPUs and ASICs in 2027-28. TSMC is on track to

reach 2026 sales guidance of slightly over 40% growth in USD (UBSe 41%), and our
growth analysis points to sustained 40% sales growth in 2027E, above consensus of
36%. We estimate Q3 sales at 14% QoQ in USD and GM at 67.1%, both at high-end of
guidance. We forecast Q4 sales to grow 10% QoQ thanks to seasonal build of flagship
phones and accelerating ramp of high performance compute (HPC), with flat GM of
67.0% considering the dilution of N2 and overseas expansion.

Further step-up of capex in 2027-28E
We have been constructive about TSMC's capex and view higher capex essential in
solidifying TSMC's leadership in advanced foundry. We keep 2026E capex at US$63bn,
and raise 2027/28E capex to US$90bn/105bn from US$80bn/95bn previously. Higher
estimates are based on: 1) our industry analysis shows that TSMC is further accelerating
expansion across N3/2 and  pulls forward tool delivery schedule of A14 for larger capacity

by 2028 when in mass production; 2) more meaningful price inflation of equipment,
and required prepayment; 3) higher capex for advanced packaging. We forecast capital
intensity to rise moderately from 36.6% in 2026 to 37.4% in 2027 but rnomalize to
32.6% in 2028. We lift TSMC's N3/2 capacity estimates at end of 2028 to
220/210kwpm, vs 200/160kwpm prior. See our foundry sector note for details.
What will the earnings call focus on?
1) Overall semi cycle outlook. UBS forecasts semis (ex memory) revenue to grow

31%/15% in 2026/27 led by Cloud AI. UBS remains conservative on consumer end
demand, estimating further 3%/4% declines for smartphone/PC units in 2027. 2)
TSMC's capex strategy over the next 3-4 years,  and whether any bottlenecks could
constrain its expansion plans. 3) Competition, especially as peers such as Samsung
Foundry and Terafab have shown increased ability to invest, although we also believe
TSMC may engage for potential angles to collaborate on Terafab. 4) Gross margin in

H226/2027. We forecast GM to decline slightly  to 67.0% in H226, and stabilize at
67.2% in 2027, underpinned by price hike, scale benefits and improved product mix.
Valuation: reiterate Buy and target of NT$3,650
We raise 2027E and beyond capex  and lift long-term earnings CAGR from 25% to 27%.
Our target of NT$3,650 is based on 21x 2027-28E PE. We see meaningful re-rating
potential from the current 16.6x/12.2x 2027/28E PE . Key catalysts include stronger

2027-28E growth outlook, continued TAM expansion and higher capex spend.

Company Description
Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing (TSMC) is the world's leading dedicated foundry
service provider, with annual capacity of about 32m of 8-inch equivalent wafers as of end-
2024. It operates four 12-inch wafer fabs, four 8-inch wafer fabs and one 6-inch wafer fab in

Taiwan. TSMC also manages two 8-inch fabs and one 12-inch fab overseas under wholly
owned subsidiaries WaferTech in the US, and TSMC China and TSMC Nanjing in China.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We base our price target for TSMC on a PE multiple.
We think TSMC faces a variety of risks, including rapidly changing technology, intense
competition, high capital investment and cyclical demand. TSMC also has a history of paying
stock options to its employees, which could dilute earnings.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.
Important Information About Sustainable Investing Strategies: Sustainable investing strategies aim to consider and incorporate environmental, social and governance
(ESG) factors into investment process and portfolio construction. Strategies across geographies approach ESG analysis and incorporate the findings in a variety of ways.
Incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing considerations may inhibit the portfolio manager’s ability to participate in certain investment opportunities that otherwise

would be consistent with its investment objective and other principal investment strategies. The returns on a portfolio incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing
considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

Greater China Technology Hardware

Industry View In-Line
In this report, we focus on China's manufacturing PMI, which we
see as a potential catalyst for AirTAC's and Hiwin's share prices.
China's manufacturing PMI edged up  to 50.1 in September from 49.8 in August:
• This came in consistent with both our estimate and consensus of 50.1.
• The production index was up 1.3ppt while the new order index was down

0.1ppt MoM.
• Our macro team believes the upturn was led mainly led by a quarter-end
production push (see their report).
Our view:
• We believe the modest pickup in the  manufacturing PMI should help
sentiment  toward relevant stocks with high exposure to the Chinese market.
Among the automation names, we prefer plays with company-specific drivers
like AirTAC and Hiwin:
° AirTAC (1590.TW, OW) should continue to benefit from its own share
gains and incremental contribution from linear guides. Valuation appears

undemanding at 17x 2027e P/E (vs. the 25x average since 2020).
° Hiwin (2049.TW, OW) should show more margin expansion in coming
quarters, supported by higher utilization and price hikes. We see
potential share price upside at the current 29x 2027e P/E vs. peak cycle
valuation of 35-40x.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
September 30, 2026 05:15 PM GMT

Valuation Methodology and Risks
AirTAC International (1590.TW)
Base case, 29x 2027e P/E. We believe this methodology better captures earnings growth
momentum for industrial automation component plays (market sentiment moves along
with business cycles). As the industry enters an up-cycle, we think applying a near-peak cycle
valuation of 29x P/E is reasonable given the stronger business outlook.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger macroeconomic climate in China

n Stronger-than-expected demand from industrial automation
Risks to Downside
n Sharper economic downturn in China
n Miniature linear guideway business development taking more time
n Larger-than-expected investment in new products but weaker-than-expected demand
Hiwin Technologies Corp. (2049.TW)
Base case, P/E. We think this valuation methodology better captures Hiwin's earnings growth
momentum, as its valuation tends to move with the business cycle. We apply a target multiple

of 38x to our 2027 EPS estimate. We view this multiple as justified by the 47% operating profit
CAGR we project for 2025-28.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger industrial automation demand pickup.
n Market share gains.
n Earlier-than-expected revenue contribution from humanoids.
Risks to Downside
n Weaker industrial automation demand.
n More pricing pressure.
n Market share loss.
n More broad-based impact on the global economy from geopolitical risks.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.

below.

Telecom & Networking Equipment  | North America
Potential FCC Rules on Optical
Transceivers More Likely to
Come in at 3.2T
Recent DC meetings highlight 3.2T as the most likely generation
to see restrictions on China-based optical transceivers. This
echoes conversations with LITE during the Q noting that any
restrictions would need to be phased in. See potential rules
keeping laser market tight, a pos for LITE/COHR.
Morgan Stanley & Co. LLC

Morgan Stanley MUFG Securities Co., Ltd.+
Yu Shirakawa
Equity Analyst
Yu.Shirakawa@morganstanleymufg.com                                                +81 3 6836-5432
Telecom & Networking Equipment
North America
Industry View In-Line
What Happened: Morgan Stanley strategists recently  met with a senior Washington
telecom, FCC and export-control legal team with direct engagement across the
White House and FCC. Discussion focused on the Administration’s efforts to reduce

reliance on Chinese technology, with the FCC Covered List emerging as the more
likely near-term mechanism for optical transceivers (a note we made in our AI Safety
DC trip wrap-up last week). Policy discussions suggest 3.2T transceivers would likely
be where restrictions would be phased in, potentially as soon as October, with
industry recently asked to demonstrate credible U.S. or friend-shoring plans before
3.2T becomes broadly deployed. Our conversations noted that discussions are also

indicating Chinese-built transceivers could still be importable, even at 3.2T, as long
as 65% of the BOM comes from US-based companies (DSP / laser would get you
pretty close). Initial 3.2T volumes are not expected to ramp in volume until 2029,
limiting the near-term revenue impact of a restriction even if action comes sooner.
We would see these rules as having the potential to avoid creating a constraint
on hyperscalers' ability to build today, but keeping the  laser market tighter given

laser would largely need to be from a US-based vendor to meet a 65% value
threshold, a positive for LITE/COHR.
How this echoes conversation with LITE earlier in the quarter: We hosted LITE at
Nasdaq Asia in late August, where we asked the company about potential FCC rules.
At the time they noted that the FCC had been in contact with them about potential
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
Recent DC trip by our strategist   team gave a greater idea of what potential FCC
rules on optical transceivers could look like.
Potential restrictions, likely to come in at 3.2T, limit Chinese-based transceivers,
with a potential workaround if US-based content above 65% of the BOM.
Solution seemingly a win-win for LITE/COHR, with laser market likely to stay

tight, but not having to take on lower module assembly business.
Also, does not create bottlenecks for hyperscalers.
Assuring InP substrate delivery will be key to look at going forward.
October 1, 2026 04:09 AM GMT

### Page 2

M
Update
2
rules, with the company noting their feedback was that the rules would need to be
phased in over time to prevent material supply/demand dislocation in the market
with 60-65% of transceivers coming from China-based transceiver vendors. They
also noted that pure transceiver volumes alone, at current pricing structures, would
not be the most attractive business for them to bring on. These rules, which are
both phased in and don't limit Chinese assemblers, as long as enough content is

US-based, would seemingly address both of these concerns.
Our Thoughts:
1. 3.2T looks like the likely policy pressure point, but the near-term impact
should be limited - Administration efforts appear focused on reshaping the
supply chain before 3.2T reaches volume rather than disrupting the existing
800G and 1.6T base. With the 3.2T ramp not expected to begin until 2028
and broader adoption closer to 2029, an October Covered List action would
provide roughly two years before meaningful volumes enter the market.

Hyperscalers are already working on plans intended to demonstrate greater
U.S. or friend-shored manufacturing, suggesting the policy objective is
increasingly about determining where the next generation is built rather
than immediately displacing current Chinese supply.
2. Laser pricing looks like the stronger read-through versus transceiver
share - A Covered List framework does not necessarily require Chinese
module vendors to lose significant share if they can increase the U.S.

content of the underlying BOM. Combining a U.S. DSP from Marvell with
laser content from LITE/COHR could potentially move a Chinese-built
transceiver to the 65% U.S.-value threshold discussed in our strategists
team policy meeting. Such an outcome would leave Innolight and Eoptolink
(both covered by Andy Meng) with less flexibility to substitute toward
China-based lasers while allowing them to remain relevant at the module
level, reducing the risk from China-based laser makers. We therefore see a

stronger read-through for high-speed laser pricing and LITE/COHR
component share than for a wholesale redistribution of transceiver share.
3. Replacement capacity not ready; nor is it the most attractive opportunity
- As noted above, LITE noted that assembly of transceivers (mid-30's to low-
40s gross margin product), is not the most attractive opportunity for US-
based optical companies (vs. EMLs and lasers well north of 50% steady
state and approaching 75% today). While 3.2T is not yet in production, we

use AAOI's current 800G and 1.6T capacity ramp as an illustration of the
scale of US supply available to absorb a potential future demand shift. AAOI
(not covered), the only material US-based production of transceivers, is
currently ramping capacity. AAOI exited Q2 with manufacturing capacity
approaching 200,000 800G and 1.6T units per month and expects to reach
~650,000 units per month by year-end 2026, while management continues
to expect demand to outpace production through mid-2027. AAOI’s YE26

capacity annualizes to ~12M 800G/1.6T units, versus ~101M units
combined for Innolight and Eoptolink based on annualized 1H capacity
(44.3M and 56.7M, respectively), or >8x AAOI. Based on AAOI’s year-end
capacity plans and our Internet team’s hyperscaler capex work, we estimate
AAOI could support less than 4 GW of hyperscaler capacity even before

### Page 3

M
Update
Morgan Stanley Research 3
factoring in manufacturing utilization. Replacing a meaningful portion of
Innolight and Eoptolink’s supply would therefore require a substantial multi-
year capacity build, with limited ability for existing U.S. suppliers to absorb
those volumes in the near term.
4. InP supply and sourcing outside of China will still be areas to watch -
Potential FCC rules could create a win-win for LITE/COHR by reducing the
risk of China-based laser makers flooding the market, without forcing them

to build significant transceiver module capacity to absorb lower-margin
assembly business. Were China to view the rules as too one-sided, it could
respond with tighter restrictions on InP substrate exports, a key bottleneck
to laser capacity ramping. At the same time, sourcing outside of China could
broaden the beneficiary set beyond U.S. suppliers, supporting InP substrate
demand at JX Advanced Metals (5016 JP), while providing a tailwind for
Sumitomo Electric’s (5802 JP) CWLD products and Furukawa Electric’s

(5801 JP) DFB lasers.

## Document 097: 261002_daiwa_nypcb

Source PDF: all report pdf/261002_daiwa_nypcb.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-02
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Summary: NYPCB released its August results (for pre-tax profit and net profit) after 
market hours on 2 October mainly due to recent volatile share price movements. Net profit 
in August 2026 came in at TWD1,092m (EPS: TWD1.69), up 665% YoY, with pre-tax profit 
at TWD1,486m (pre-tax margin at 27.8%). The August pre-tax margin of 27.8% is close to 
our current forecast (28.5%/32.9% in 3Q26E/4Q26E). As we expect limited impact from 
non-ops and on opex in August, we infer the gross margin in August should have reached 
to c.30-31% (vs. our forecast c.30.3/34.4% in 3Q26E/4Q26E). This echoes our previous 
notes (Spec upgrade to drive additional ABF substrate demand); we expect NYPCB’s 
gross margin will recover to c.30% after Kunshan’s yield rate improvement in August. As 
such, we believe the August results to be largely on track with market expectations. 
However, we believe the share price upside will come from BT substrate, where we should 
see better progress in its implementing price hikes now (vs. limited visibility earlier in 
September). We expect NYPCB to raise ASP of BT substrates by single digits QoQ in 
4Q26 and +20% QoQ in 1Q27. This is aligned with our previous comment (Clarification on 
“no intention to hike price from 4Q26”) that we expect BT substrate prices will accelerate 
again in 2027 despite a lower-than-expected price hike in 4Q26E. We view NYPCB’s 
August results announcement as a near-term price catalyst. We have a Buy rating (1) on 
NYPCB with a 12-month TP of TWD2,460. Downside risk: weaker-than-expected demand 
for switches/PCs/ smartphones.

研 究  報 告                
中磊(5388.TT) 
產業：電子–通信網路 
三大產品線皆穩健成⻑，惟 2027 年記憶體供需仍持續緊俏 
 投資評價與建議 
看好中磊受惠10G PON、Wi-Fi 7與DOCSIS 4.0網通規格升級以及5G FWA放
量，預估2026/2027年稅後EPS為5.72/7.92元，過往中磊forward PE區間多落
在9.5~15倍，雖然今明年訂單能見度佳，惟考量1H26逐步採用高成本記憶體
生產，同時2027年記憶體供需仍緊俏，故從2027年獲利評價，給予11倍PER。  
 重點評論分析 
Wi-Fi 7規格全面導入，2Q26營收大幅增加，惟毛利率受記憶體漲價承壓：
2Q26營收季增23.9%至206.2億元，係因轉嫁記憶體漲價成本以及Wi-Fi 7規
格產品全面放量 ， 毛利率季減1.36ppts ， 係受記憶體漲價影響 ， 稅後EPS為1.58
元 。 2Q26季底存貨金額較1Q26增加約130億元 ， 其中約80億元為記憶體成品 ，
用於滿足3Q26訂單需求。隨著第三季進入網通出貨旺季，預估3Q26營收季增
9.5%至225.8億元，稅後EPS為1.52元。 
三大產品線均穩健成⻑ ， 惟2027年記憶體供需持續緊俏 ：展望2026~2027年 ，
營收動能來自 ： (1)Broadband CPE產品受惠Fiber PON產品往10G以上規格迭
代，Cable DOCSIS從3.1往4.0升級以及5G FWA出貨量增加；(2)DAA分散式
局端設備目前大型設備商客戶認證時程遞延，預估1Q27將逐步開始出貨，有
利提升後續毛利率表現；(3)Enterprise產品則受惠Wi-Fi 7滲透率提升，目前
Wi-Fi 7比重已達無線產品出貨量之70%。 
 投資風險 
記憶體等零組件漲價與缺料。

### Page 2

2

| 營運概況  
Wi-Fi 7規格全面導入，2Q26營收大幅增加，惟毛利率受記憶體漲價承壓，進入出貨旺季，3Q26營收持續走升：
2Q26營收季增23.9%至206.2億元 ，營收成⻑中約有⼀半來自記憶體成本轉嫁客戶以及Wi-Fi 7規格產品全面放量 ，
毛利率大幅季減1.36ppts，係受記憶體漲價影響，營業費用率因營收規模擴大而季減1.71ppts至9%，營業利益率
季增0.3ppts至3.3%，稅後EPS為1.58元。資產負債表方面，2Q26季底存貨金額較1Q26增加約130億元，其中約
80億元為記憶體，主要用於滿足3Q26訂單需求。由於記憶體缺貨將造成下游電信營運商客戶無法正常出貨，進而
導致消費者轉往其他同業，將喪失市佔率，故確保「正常交貨」成為網通廠和電信營運商首要目標，公司亦持續
與客戶協商轉嫁成本。隨第三季進入網通出貨旺季，預估3Q26營收季增9.5%至225.8億元，稅後EPS為1.52元。 
三大產品線均穩健成⻑ ， 惟2027年記憶體供需持續緊俏 ：展望2026~2027年，中磊營收動能來自：(1)Broadband 
CPE產品受惠Fiber PON產品往10G以上規格迭代 ， 同時近期中磊領先於同業於SCTE TechExpo26發表50G PON ，
提供客戶下世代光纖產品，另外Cable DOCSIS逐步從3.1往4.0規格升級以及5G FWA出貨量增加；(2)DAA分散式
局端設備透過將大局端設備到⼩節點間改鋪光纖，最後⼀哩路才採用Cable，用於解決訊號衰減與延遲之問題，目
前大型設備商客戶認證時程遞延，預估1Q27將逐步開始出貨，有利提升後續毛利率表現；(3)Enterprise產品受惠
Wi-Fi 7規格滲透率持續提升，目前Wi-Fi 7比重已達無線產品出貨量之70%。綜上所述，預估2026/2027年營收年
增53.9%/14.1%至831/948.4億元，稅後EPS為5.72/7.92元。

M31(6643 TT)  
台灣股市︱電子上游 
投 資 速 報 
買進-首次 目標價 NT$666 (潛在報酬+36.0%) 2026-09-23

先進製程滲透，推升 IP 與 Royalty    
◼ 結 論 與 建 議 
AI/HPC 與 Chiplet 推升高速 Inter face IP 需 求 ， 公 司 布 局 PCIe Gen 6/7 、
112G SerDes 及 D2D （ 晶粒間高速互連 ），受惠 AI 伺服器高速互連需求成
長。預估 M31(6643 T T)  2026 年 EPS 5.83 元， 2027 年 2/3nm 先進製程
Royalty 放 量 、 占 比 目 標 突 破 20% ， 成 為 營 收 成 長 與 獲 利 率 改 善 主 要 動 能 。
EPS 15.85 元，建 議 給 予 本 益 比 42 倍，設 定 目 標 價 666 元（42X ＊ 2027(F) ），
評 等 「 買 進 」 。

◼ 重點摘 要  
AI/HPC 與 Chiplet 驅 動 高 速 介面  IP 需求  
隨 AI/HPC 、資料中心及 Chiplet 架構發展， AI 系 統 效 能 瓶 頸 逐 步 由 運 算 能
力延伸至 Memor y 與 I/O 頻寬，帶動 PCIe 、 SerDes 、 D2D 及 DDR 等 高 速
Inter face IP 需 求 提 升 ； 大 型 單 晶 SoC 逐 步 朝 多 Die ／ Chiplet 架構發展，
透過不同製程 Die 整 合 提 高 頻 寬 、 良 率 與 設 計 彈 性 ， 並 增 加 Die -to -Die 高
速互連需求。 IPne st 預 估 ， 全 球 高 階 Inter face IP 市場規模將由 2025 年
25.7 億 美 元 增 至 2029 年 49.0 億 美 元 ， CAGR 17.5% ，其中 Ethernet ／ D2D
／ SerDes CAGR 約 21% 成長最快， PCIe 及 DDR 亦約 17% 。

公 司 簡 介 — 全 球 基 礎 元 件 與 高 速 傳 輸 介 面 IP 廠  
M31 主 要 提 供 半 導 體 矽 智 財（ IP ）， 產 品 涵 蓋 基 礎 元 件 、 高 速 傳 輸 介 面 及 類
比 IP ，主要服務全球晶圓代工廠與 IC 設 計 公 司 。 產 品 包 括 標 準 元 件 庫 、記
憶 體 編 譯 器 （ Memor y Compiler ） 、 輸 入 輸 出 介 面 （ I/O ） 、 靜 態 隨 機 存 取
記 憶 體 （SRAM ） ， 以 及 通 用 序 列 匯 流 排 （USB ） 、 高 速 周 邊 元 件 互 連 （PCIe ）、
行 動 產 業 處 理 器 介 面 （ MIPI ） 、 顯 示 介 面 （ Displ ayPor t ） 、 低 功 耗 記 憶 體
介 面 （ LPDDR ） 、 快 閃 記 憶 體 介 面 （ONFI ） 、 通 用 快 閃 儲 存 介 面（ UFS ）、
高 速 串 列 收 發 器（ SerDes ）等 高 速 介 面 IP ， 應 用 涵 蓋 AI/HPC 、資料中心、
網通、儲存、手機、車用及 AIoT 。隨先進製程設計複雜度與成本提升，第
三方 IP 採 用 需 求 增 加 ； 公 司 持 續 由 成 熟 製 程 向 2 ～ 16nm 先 進 製 程 延 伸 ， 並
與 台積電、聯電、 Intel 、 Sa msung 、 GlobalFoundries 等晶圓廠合作。

下半年 成長 加溫  
M31  2Q2026 營收 4.2 億 元 ， QoQ +6.9% ， YoY -6% ，營益率 -4% ， QoQ 
+1 2.8ppts ， YoY -12.7ppts ， EPS -0.0 6 元。 2Q26 營 運 明 顯 改 善 ， 受 惠 營
收回升、雲端運算支出下降及研發資源配置優化，營益率 改善至 -4.0% ，
EPS 收斂至 -0.0 6 元 。 預 估 M31  3Q2026 營收 5.5 億元， QoQ +31.5% ，
YoY +56.4% ， 營 益 率 18.9% ， QoQ +22.9ppts ， YoY +32.5ppts ， EPS 
2.25 元。 1H26 完 成 全 年 原 規 劃 40% 營收 ， 下 半 年 目 標 完 成 60% ， 以 全 年
營收雙位數成長為目標 。展望 2027 年，受 惠 Royalty 持續成長及費用 控 管，
營收與獲利率可望同步改善 。

2nm 完成 Tape -out

獻； Fabless 占營收 81% 、 創 近 五 季 高 ， 涵 蓋 智 慧 終 端 、 AIoT 、 記 憶 體 及 車
用晶片，其中 16nm 以 下 授 權 金 占 Fabless 達 68.3% 。 隨 2nm 客戶已完成
Tape -out ，預計 4Q26 ～ 1Q27 進 入 量 產 並 貢 獻 Royalty 。

拓 展 北 美 CS P、 合作 ASI C 業者  
公司由消費性 IP 延伸至 AI/HPC 與 Data Center ，布局 PCIe Gen 6/7 、
112G SerDes 及 高 階 SRAM ，並透過 Design Ser vice 拓展北美 CSP 、與台
灣主要 ASIC 業 者 洽 談 3nm 等 新 案 ； 另 與 信 驊 科 技 深 化 伺 服 器 Inter face 及
Customized  IP 合作，既有合作涵蓋 BMC 及 Cupola 3D 相機，其中
Cupola360 已採用 MIPI D -PHY IP 。

免責 宣言 :  
本 投 資 報 告 內 容 屬 康 和 投 資 顧 問 股 份 有 限 公 司 版 權 所 有 ， 禁 止 任 何 形 式 之 抄 襲 、 引 用 或 轉 載 。 本 研 究 報 告 所 載 內 容 ， 純 屬
研 究 性 質 ， 所 載 述 意 見 可 隨 時 予 以 更 改 或 撤 回 並 不 另 行 通 知 ， 僅 提 供康和 集團內部 同 仁 及 客 戶 參 考 ， 文 中 所 載 資 訊 或 任何
意 見 ， 不 構 成 ( 且 不 應 解 釋 為 ) 在 任 何 司 法 管 轄 區 內 ， 任 何 買 賣 有 價 證 劵 或 其 他 投 資 標 的 之 要 約 、 招 攬 、 邀 請 、 宣 傳 、 誘 使 ，
或 任 何 不 論 種 類 或 形 式 之 表 示 、 建 議 或推 薦 買 賣 本 研 究 報 告 所 述 的 任 何 證 劵 。 報 告 內 容 所 提 及 之各項 業務 、財務 等 相 關 資
訊、 意 見 及 預 估 ， 係取材自 據 信 為 可 靠 之 資 料 來 源 ， 既 不 以 明 示 或 默 示 的 方 式 ， 對 資 料 之 準 確 性 、 完 整 性 或 正 確 性 做 出任
何 陳 述 或 保 證 ， 且 報 告 係 基 於 特 定 日 期 所 做 之 判 斷 ， 有 其 時 效 性 限 制 ， 如 有 變 更 ， 本 公 司 將 不 做 預 告 或 更 新 。 對 於 本 投 資
報 告 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 任 何 證 劵 、 投 資 標 的 ， 或 文 中所討論 或 建 議 之 投 資 策 略 ， 投 資 人 於 決 策 時 應 審 慎 衡 量 本 身 風 險 ， 並 就
投 資 結 果 自 行 負 責。

Goldilocks 
We resume coverage of the PCB/CCL industry, focusing primarily on Google’s TPU supply 
chain, which we believe will deliver the strongest and most sustainable growth through 2028 
and beyond. We forecast aggregate TPU volumes to more than triple between 2025/26 and 
2028, far outpacing competing platforms. Critically, we project a surge in the content value 
of next-generation TPU ASICs. As Google integrates more advanced logic and HBM chiplets 
into a single compute module, we project TPU content value to increase five- to tenfold over 
the next three to four years.  Accordingly, we estimate that the addressable PCB content 
value alone could quadruple from 2026 to 2029. Equally vital is Google’s longstanding 
leadership in optical connectivity for its Superpods. The upcoming doubling of switch speeds 
and interconnect bandwidth will act as a secondary tailwind, further lifting PCB content 
value. Consequently, we expect the PCB/CCL market to experience continuous pricing power 
and a possibility of LTAs forming, mirro ring the supply dynamics seen in the memory 
industry. Within the key TPU vendor matrix, we resume coverage of Isu Petasys and EMC with 
BUY ratings and target prices of KRW 270k and TWD 10,000, respectively. We also initiate 
coverage of TTMI with a BUY rating and a target price of $236. Risks include end demand, 
execution, and US policy on AI data centre expansion. 
Why Google’s TPUs?  
We forecast TPU’s volume to more than triple to 12m units in 2028 (8.5mn in 2027) from 3.6mn 
in 2026, encompassing both in-house deployment and allocations to third-party AI labs via ASIC 
services. The strong volume is evident by its massive $520bn cloud service backlog orders in 
C2Q26. At the same time, TPU is expected to quadruple in TPU V9 (mid-28) vs V8 (2H26) and 
could double again in V10 (2029) as Google squeezes more chiplets into its ASIC designs. The 
complexity of design for PCB (upgrade to 50L+ or HDI) and CCL (upgrade to M8/M9 material or 
even PTFE) represents at least 50%-100% expansion in ASP in each generation. Furthermore, 
networking is expected to see a similar upgrade trend in PCB/CCL, as scale-up architectures 
and bandwidth requirement rise, especially from 400Gbps to 800G/1.6Tbps.    
LTA likely becomes new norm under strong demand and supply constraint  
Our supply chain survey indicated increasing discussion about long-term agreements (LTA) 
over the PCB and CCL supply chain with CSPs. Terms may be different from vendor to vendor, 
and most of them are still under discussion at this stage. However, those discussions send a 
clear message that end users are securing high-end PCB/CCL capacity by prepayment and LTAs 
for the next few years, because the overall industry may continue to remain undersupplied. 
Also, we reckon prepayment 1) will ease supply chain pressure on capex/cash flow, 2) make the 
supply chain more willing to invest given longer visibility and secured shipment, and 3) improve 
supply chain management with better profitability. We believe this will further boost P/E for the 
supply chain, if LTAs are confirmed, with longer visibility and stable order backlogs.   
We prefer TPU supply chain into 2027/28: buy on EMC, ISU and TTM 
The TPU supply chain will be the biggest winner into 2027/28 with strong unit growth and 
content value upgrades. Shipments are expected to reach 8.5/12m in 2027/28 vs 3.6m in 2026. 
Content value per rack for PCBs is up to USD30k-40k and CCL USD12k-18k for TPU v8 (vs 
USD20k/7k in v7). We expect another 40%–50% in v9 with HDI adoption. We like EMC for its 
leading position and expect TPU/800Gbps switches to become the company’s key earnings 
driver. We set our TP at NT$10,000, with 30x FY27/28 PER. ISU is uniquely positioned to capture 
these secular trends due to its high exposure to Google and networking. We have a BUY and a 
TP of KRW270,000 on 30x FY27/28 PER. Lastly, TTM is the best proxy in the United States to 
capture both AI server and national defence trends with better profitability and irreplaceability 
given its industry position. We initiate with BUY with a TP of USD236 with 30x FY27/28 PER. Title

GPU/ASIC volume, content value and price hikes drive 
PCB/CCL to another strong cycle in 2027/28 
We are positive on the PCB/CCL sector into 2027/28, with a strong upgrade cycle, and the 
whole industry will remain undersupplied because our checks indicate 1) continued AI device 
unit growth, supported by increasing CSP capex, 2) PCB design complexity will boost content 
value (increasing processing time and lowering yield rate), 3) CCL material upgrade to 
support higher data transmission and power supply, and 4) price hikes in the near-term 
because of the supply shortage and LTAs in the longer-term for a better demand outlook. 
What is not in the price yet?  
The sector has outperformed year-to-date, but we believe the PCB/CCL upgrade cycle will 
continue to surprise the market because 1) stronger AI device unit growth (especially Google 
TPU, of which we project 8.5/12m units in 2027/28), 2) significant content value growth via 
PCB design complexity (higher layer counts, advanced form factor like HDI/mSAP, longer 
processing time) and CCL material upgrades (M8/M9, PTFE), 3) wider AI end devices (GPU, 
ASIC, switch, CPU and LPU, for example) will all require high end PCB s to support  
performance and 4) better cash flow and profitability via prepayment and LTAs.  
The overall industry is under a severe supply shortage at this stage, especially high-end 
products, with the key bottleneck being in upstream material (CCL, glass fiber and copper foil) 
as well as PCB equipment, based on our supply chain checks. The longest lead-time for 
certain PCB equipment (like drilling machines) can be up to 36 months, suggesting strong 
surge in demand from the supply chain. We don’t see any sign of the tightness easing in the 
near term.  
AI server unit growth and spec upgrade in 2027/28   
We estimate GPU & ASIC volume will grow approximately 50% in CY2027 and CY2028, 
reaching a potential 34mn units in CY28, up from 15m units in CY26. The key driver is the 
expected unit surge in both CSPs’ ASICs as well as AI labs, that is, OpenAI and Anthropic. Their 
aggregate volume is forecast to rise from 5.5mn –6mn in CY26 to reach 12mn -13mn units in 
CY26 and further increase to 20mn in CY28, implying a quadrupling in a three-year period. 
Some of the notable drivers including the following: 
▶OpenAI’s direct procurement compute power (excluding CPS and neoclouds) is expected 
to reach 11Gw in CY28, up from 3.3GW in CY27 and a mere 1GB in CY26. This includes AVGO’s-
sponsored ASIC of 1.3GW (Jalapeno) and 5GW in CY27/28 respectively, while the remaining 
goes to AMD (8GW in the three-year period) and, to a lesser extent, NVDA; 
▶Similarly, Anthropic’s own compute capacity is likely to reach 13GW in CY28, up sharply 
from CY27’s 6GW. The majority of this comes from AVGO (TPU V8i and V8.5) while 5GW is 
supplied by AMD of  in two years; 
▶Meta is likely to build 3GW of its own compute on its ASIC (via AVGO) in CY27/28 using its 
MTIA400/450 ASIC. We note Meta’s previous ASIC program in 2025/26 has been scaled back 
and pushed out so this marks the second attempt at ramping up its own silicon solution.   
▶We estimate Google’s own compute TPU volume is expected to increase to 5.5mn units in 
CY28 from 4mn -4.5mn in CY27 and ~3.5mn in both CY25/26. While the unit growth appears to 
be modest relative to the AI labs, we note the compute capacity is likely to surge given the 
TDP for V8 and V9 is materially higher than those of V6/7.  
While NVDA’s GPU volume is likely to moderate and market share is expected to decline (as 
most of its customers are building their own ASIC and diversifying to other suppliers), it will 
remain the dominant supplier to most CSPs, neoclouds and enterprises. Moreover, NVDA, 
along with industry is migrating to a multi-rack or large pod structure to offer aggregated

AI server performance to jump significantly every generation 
As shown in the table below, we see huge upgrades in compute power and TDP every 
generation. PCBs and CCL will upgrade along with AI server specs to reduce data loss and 
improve signal performance. PCB design is getting more complex from 20L -30L to 50L+ in the 
future or the structure will likely be migrated from HLT to HDI. For CCL, we witness a clear 
trend of upgrade material from M7 to M8 now and will see more adoption of the M8.5/M9 
going forward, boosting the ASP (a doubling from M7 to M8; M8 to M9 also doubling).  
In the future, along with strong agentic AI demand, we believe AI server design will further 
advance exponentially in the next two to three years. TPU’s TDP is expected to grow 10x from 
v8 to v10 within two to three years to further drive-up PCB/CCL’s content value significantly. 
AMD will equip PCBs with 50L+ into the MI500 generation and Open AI’s aggressive hardware 
production pipeline will provide upside surprise to the market. We can expect PCB/CCL to 
upgrade accordingly. We will have a more comprehensive analysis in the next section.

Summary: PCB/CCL spec upgrade and key suppliers in AI solutions  
The table below shows the key players’ solution and their PCB/CCL specifications as well as 
major suppliers. For PCB, we see an increasing layer count from 20L–30L now to 50L+ in the 
coming years and moving from HLC to HDI for more complex design. Our supply chain check 
suggests AMD’s MI500 series as well as Nvidia’s LPU PCB will likely adopt 50L+ design. This 
represents a longer processing time with a lower yield rate, and design complexity will 
support further ASP hikes. For CCL, M8 is becoming a standard material for AI 
servers/networking and we are going to see more advanced materials into 2027/28 (M9/PTFE). 
CCL is the most critical component in PCB and every generation upgrade (M7 to M8, or M8 to 
M9) implies the ASP will nearly double.    
Nvidia: It started to adopt M8 material from the GB200 NVL72. Rubin is expected to bring 
another modest upgrade: 1) introducing mid-plane with 44L design and M8 (or M8.5) CCL 
material, 2) most of the rest PCB/CCL remain on M8 with higher layer counts. The company is 
evaluating PTFE material for its switch board in the next generation. Also, we think Nvidia’s 
LPU can generate incremental demand for PCB/CCL with its 52L design with M9/M8 materials. 
PCB suppliers for LPU will be VGT, WUS and TTM mainly, while, in our view, CCL suppliers are 
Shengyi (major) and EMC.  
Google: TPU is expected to see the biggest upside in the PCB/CCL addressable market in 
2027/28 thanks to both unit growth and content value upgrades. The next section will have a 
comprehensive analysis.  
Amazon: Trn 3’s air-cooled solution will remain at a 26L PCB design (with M8 material), while 
the liquid-cooled version will migrate to 40L. for Trn3 is solely EMC and TUC are the only CCL 
suppliers for the Trn 3. We expect to see a more significant upgrade for PCB/CCL in the next 
generation Trn 4 (higher layer counts, upgrade to M9?) 
AMD: AMD is another key growth driver for the sector, with meaningful volume into 2027/28. 
The MI450, with 48L/M8 design, will start to contribute from next year and we believe AMD’s 
MI500 series will likely adopt a 50L+ PCB design with M9 materials. This will represent a huge

Google’s TPU to undergo rapid spec upgrades in next 
several years; its PCB/CCL will benefit accordingly  
The figures below illustrate the rapid production transition and specificiation advancement 
of Google’s TPU roadmap in the next several years. We highlight the following considerations: 
▶ The current V8i variant, which is undergoing HVM (through 1H28), receives a modest 
upgrade vs TPU V7 in terms of design and specification. For the latter, there are two logic dies 
and four HBM stacks encompassing TDP of approximately 1,000w. In TPU V8i, the TPD rises 
by 30% with the addition of one logic tile and two HBM stacks.  
▶ We expect the next generation of TPU V9 to undergo a massive uplift in design complexity, 
TPD and compute capability. We estimate there are 12 logic dies (4x times more than V8i) and 
12 HBM stacks (double that of V8i) to be the first to useTSMC’s N2 process node.  Given the 
rapid rise in chiplet quantity, we estimate the reticle size of the design to be 9.5x reticle size. 
The compute capability is likely to surge accordingly with the TDP of both training and 
inference to reach 4,500w, more than triple compared to TPU V8i.  We expect TPU V9 to begin 
HBM in mid-2028 and run through 2029. 
▶ We think TPU V10 will experience the next wave of significant specification and 
performance uplift as the number of logic chiplets rises further to 30, 2.5x vs TPU V9 and they 
are all to be fabicated on TSMC’s N2P geometry as well as being encompassed with 3D 
packaging for the first time. In addition, the number of HBM stacks will rise by one-third to 16 
stacks. We estimate the reticle size is likely to increase to 15x, 50% larger than TPU V9 and 
critically the TDP to surge to 10,000w if not higher!. TPU V10 is expected to enter HBM in 2029.

Implications for PCB/CCL 
We think the TPU supply chain will enjoy the biggest growth in 2027/28 along with strong unit 
growth and content value upgrades among all the GPU/ASICs. Shipments are expected to 
reach 8.5m/12m in 2027/28 vs 3.6m in 2026. We view increasing compute power, higher HBM 
density, and a doubling of ICI bandwidth as pushing PCB designs toward significantly higher 
routing density, tighter L/S, more advanced CCL, smaller via structures, and multi-stage 
lamination to maintain signal integrity and shorten electrical paths.  
We observed that TPU’s PCB ASP has risen 2.5x from TPU V5 to V8, and 4x for the 400Gbps 
switch to 800Gbps. In addition, we estimate 2.6x growth in TPU unit demand from 2025E-27E 
and 3x expansion in the 400bps/800Gbps AI switch market by 2028 versus 2025, or 30% 
growth in total, making TAM growth exponential in these years. 
The table below illustrates the structure of Google TPU v8. One rack will contain eight 
compute trays, with each tray equipped with four TPUs, four NICs, four optical modules as 
well as two CPUs (Axiom). TOR (Top-Of-Rack)/OCS will connect those racks together to form 
a superpod (via the Virgo network) and the Juniper network to connect with other functions. 
The increasing compute power and connectivity represents significant content value growth 
for PCB/CCL based on those performance upgrades and data transmission.  
To support the performance upgrades, TPU PCB architecture is shifting from standard 
multilayer (Ghostfish) to N+M structures using dual -grade CCL and multi-lamination in 
Zebrafish and Sunfish, and ultimately to HDI PCB in Humufish. These transitions lift PCB ASPs 
by 30% in Ghostfish, 80%–90% in Sunfish/Zebrafish. Based on our estimates, content value 
per rack for PCB is up to USD30k-40k and CCL USD12-18k for TPU v8 (vs USD20k/7k in 
v7). We expect another 40%–50% in Humufish with HDI adoption. Into TPU V10, given 
that TDP will further double to 10kW, we can assume PCB/CCL will be further upgraded 
to meet the higher requirement.

N+M sequential lamination is inherently more challenging than a standard 
multilayer build because it requires multiple lamination cycles, yield rates could 
fall by 15%-30%. 
HDI PCB requires fewer layers owing to 
its high density, but it is more challenging 
than N+M because it requires multiple 
cycles of laser drilling, sequential 
buildup and ultra-fine feature formation. 
That would see further yield rate loss. 
ASP Index 1 1.3 2.2 2.1 2.5 ~5-6 
Source: Aletheia/TAG 
We see an increasing trend for adopting HDI in advanced AI acclerators: 1) it shortens high-
frequency signal paths, reducing signal attenuation and latency, 2) it increases circuit density 
through microvia technology, including blind and buried vias, 3) it saves internal space and 
4) it optimizes heat dissipation efficiency. HDI normally requires fewer layers compared to 
conventinal multi-lam PCB due to its higher density, but HDI PCB is still more challenging 
because it requires multiple cycles of laser drilling, sequential buildup, and ultra-fine feature 
formation. The longer processing time and lower yield rate will further drive up content value 
for PCB.

Increasing networking growth will be another pillar of growth 
ToR (Top‑of‑Rack) switch demand for AI server systems is set to grow the fastest during 2026–
28 as architectures scale-up and bandwidth requirements rise. As scale ‑up bandwidth 
increases, data move outward more quickly, shifting bottlenecks in the scale‑out network. 
Google’s TPU v8 network architecture is a good example as illustrated below.  
FIGURE 12: MLCC DEMAND IN VARIOUS AI SERVERS AND GENERAL SERVERS 
Source: Aletheia/TAG 
Adoption of 800 Gbps switches is set to accelerate in 2026–27, with CCL moving from M7 to 
M8 and PCB architecture shifting from multilayer to N+M. As a result, we estimate the 800 
Gbps switch PCB ASP will rise ~275% to ~USD1,500 from USD350 –400 in 400 Gbps 
systems. The layer count will increase by 35%–40%, with the remaining value uplift driven 
by higher‑grade materials and more complex structures. For 1.6T switches, PCB ASP is

expected to reach USD3,000–4,000 as the layer count expands to 44–50L and CCL upgrades 
to M9 (potentially mixed with M8 in power layers). 
At the Hot Chips Conference, a premier annual semiconductor industry event, at Stanford 
University at the end of August, Google further disclosed the networking architecture for its 
upcoming TPU8t (Zebrafish – MediaTek) and TPU8i (Sunfish – Broadcom). In addition to 
upgrading its Jupiter network (north-south traffic) architecture, which connects storage, 
general servers, and its global WAN with higher bandwidth, lower latency, and fewer hops, 
Google also detailed its new Virgo network ( east-west traffic). Through the co-design of 
network architecture, optical switching technology, and the software orchestration layer, the 
Virgo network facilitates scaling the maximum TPU cluster size to 1m units (up from 100,000 
units in TPU v7 – Ironwood).  
This upgrade highly relies on networking hardware advancements, particularly in optical 
communications. Our deep-dive research into Google’s new TPU network architecture leads 
to the conclusion that demand will increase for ethernet switches and optical transceivers.

Who will benefit most in the Google TPU supply chain?  
ISU Petasys is best positioned to capture this growth given its high exposure to networking 
PCBs (50%–60% of sales) and its role as a key PCB supplier to Google (50%+ of sales). We also 
consider EMC another great proxy for the TPU supply chain, because  of 1) its leading position 
in high-end CCL, 2) its increasing market share in ASICs (Google, AWS) as well as AMD, and 3) 
its better margin/profitability along with favorable product mix and price hike s. TTM is 
another key supplier to Google TPU for N+M products, and we expect its business to 
accelerate from 2H26. If Google confirms LTAs with its PCB/CCL vendors, we think it will 
further drive-up the supply chain’s P/E with more stable backlogs and longer order visibility.  
Price hikes will continue; LTAs are ongoing  
Another key growth driver to lift PCB/CCL makers’ profitability is price hikes. We have 
witnessed several rounds of price hikes already this year and expect more. Besides, our 
channel check suggests that CSPs are working closely with PCB/CCL makers for LTAs to 
secure advanced capacity and smooth the production process. This section will discuss these 
two issues and implications for the supply chain.  
Price hikes will likely continue  
Our supply chain checks suggest that CCL makers are increasing prices by 50%-100% YTD to 
reflect 1) organic material upgrades under the supply constraint, 2) a shortage inf upstream 
materials supply and price hikes (resin, copper foil and glass cloth), and 3) longer and more 
complex laminate process for more advanced CCL. We see price hikes across the board from 
all production lines from M8 material to M4  because tier 1 makers are allocating more 
capacity for advanced materials (which requires a longer processing time resulting in lower 
production volume). As such, entry-level products will be excluded and will partially shift to 
second-tier vendors.  
Based on current visibility, we expect more waves of price hikes in CCL in 2H26 and 1H27. We 
think each round will be 10%-15%. We assume better profitability for CCL makers in future 
quarters owing to 1) higher UTR under a supply constraint, 2) continued price hikes and 3) 
favorable product mix along with product migration.  
Are LTAs becoming new norm for PCB/CCL?  
Given the tight supply conditions and strong PCB/CCL demand for the foreseeable future, we 
are also hearing that CSPs are aggressively approaching PCB and CCL makers for LTAs to 
secure capacity. Terms will vary from vendor to vendor. However, those discussions send a 
clear message that end users are securing high-end PCB/CCL capacity by prepayment and 
LTAs for the next few years, because the overall industry may continue to remain 
undersupplied. Also, we reckon prepayment 1) will ease supply chain pressure on capex/cash 
flow, 2) make the supply chain more willing to invest under longer order visibility and secured 
shipment volume, and 3) improve supply chain management with better profitability. Below 
are typical LTAs between memory suppliers and their key customers.
